{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/vc-x1/src/scope.rs",
  "old_string": "    #[test]\n    fn empty_roles() {\n        let s = Scope::Roles(vec![]);\n        assert!(s.is_empty());\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn single_helpers_all_false() {\n        // `Single(_)` is a distinct mode; the Roles-shaped helpers\n        // all report false / not-empty so callers that match on\n        // helpers naturally fall through to mode-aware logic.\n        let s = Scope::Single(PathBuf::from(\"/some/repo\"));\n        assert!(!s.has_code());\n        assert!(!s.has_bot());\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n        assert!(!s.is_empty());\n    }",
  "new_string": "    #[test]\n    fn empty_roles() {\n        // Empty side list isn't a state anything constructs (the\n        // parser rejects empty input), but the helpers still need\n        // to be well-defined on it.\n        let s = Scope::Roles(vec![]);\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn single_helpers_all_false() {\n        // `Single(_)` is a distinct mode; the Roles-shaped helpers\n        // all report false so callers that match on helpers\n        // naturally fall through to mode-aware logic.\n        let s = Scope::Single(PathBuf::from(\"/some/repo\"));\n        assert!(!s.has_code());\n        assert!(!s.has_bot());\n        assert!(!s.is_code_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_bot_only());\n        assert!(!s.is_both());\n    }"
}

  "new_string": "Build is warning-clean; 257 tests pass (255 baseline + 2\nnew in scope/common).\n\n### 0.42.0-2: --scope parser + retrofit init\n\nLands the custom CLI value parser and migrates `init` onto\nthe new `Scope` shape. Parser is the shared boundary every\nremaining `--scope` consumer (sync, push, finalize, clone)\nwill route through in -3+; init is the smallest first\nconsumer and exercises the keyword path end-to-end. The\npath form is accepted by the parser but rejected at init\nwith a hint pointing at `--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`\n(init creates a workspace, not a single-repo project).\n\n- `src/scope.rs`: `pub fn parse_scope(s: &str) -> Result<Scope, String>`.\n  Accepts the four keyword forms (`code`, `bot`, `code,bot`,\n  `bot,code`) plus path forms prefixed by `./`, `../`, `/`,\n  `~/`, or the bare `~`. Anything else errors with a hint\n  pointing at the prefix-disambiguation rule. Order is\n  preserved on the keyword forms. Drops the now-dead\n  `is_empty` helper (parser rejects empty input upstream;\n  the only consumer that called it was init's redundant\n  validation). 13 new parser unit tests.\n- `src/init.rs`: `--scope` field flips from\n  `Option<Vec<Side>>` (clap `value_delimiter = ','`) to\n  `Option<Scope>` with `value_parser = crate::scope::parse_scope`.\n  `plan_init` matches on the parsed `Scope`: `Roles(_)`\n  takes the existing bot-only-fatal path; `Single(_)` is\n  rejected with the init-specific hint. Test scaffolding\n  (`fixture_scoped`) and 5 fixture call sites migrated.\n  `scope_parses_*` tests retitled around the new field\n  type. New `scope_path_form_rejected_at_init` test pins\n  the rejection contract.\n\nSmoke-tested end-to-end via CLI:\n\n- `vc-x1 init tf1 --scope code --dry-run` → succeeds\n  (single-repo dry-run plan).\n- `vc-x1 init tf1 --scope code,bot --dry-run` → succeeds.\n- `vc-x1 init tf1 --scope bot --dry-run` → bot-only fatal\n  (existing `plan_init` check).\n- `vc-x1 init tf1 --scope ./foo --dry-run` → path-form\n  fatal (new `plan_init` check).\n- `vc-x1 init tf1 --scope '~/work' --dry-run` → path-form\n  fatal.\n- `vc-x1 init tf1 --scope foo --dry-run` → clap-level\n  parser error with hint about `./foo` disambiguation."
}

> AGENT

Work complete for 0.42.0-2.

**Cargo.toml** — `0.42.0-1` → `0.42.0-2`.

**src/scope.rs** — added `pub fn parse_scope(s: &str) -> Result<Scope, String>`:
- Keyword forms: exactly one of `code`, `bot`, `code,bot`, `bot,code` → `Roles(...)`, order preserved.
- Path forms: `./X`, `../X`, `/X`, `~/X`, bare `~` → `Single(PathBuf)`, raw string stored.
- Anything else errors with a hint about `./X` disambiguation.
- Dropped the dead-after-this-step `is_empty` helper (parser rejects empty upstream).
- 13 new parser tests; existing `single_helpers_all_false` / `empty_roles` tests pruned to no longer assert on `is_empty`.
- Removed `#[allow(dead_code)]` from `Single` (parser now constructs it).

**src/init.rs**:
- `--scope` field: `Option<Vec<Side>>` (with `value_delimiter`) → `Option<Scope>` (with `value_parser = crate::scope::parse_scope`). Help text updated to note the path form is parser-accepted but init-rejected.
- `plan_init` matches on the parsed enum: `Roles(_)` keeps the bot-only-fatal check; `Single(p)` errors with a hint pointing at `--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`.
- `fixture_scoped` signature: `Option<Vec<Side>>` → `Option<Scope>`. 5 fixture sites and the two `scope_parses_*` tests migrated. New `scope_path_form_rejected_at_init` test.

**notes/chores-07.md** — added `### 0.42.0-2: --scope parser + retrofit init` subsection.

**notes/todo.md** — In Progress: `-1 (done)`, `-2 (current)`.

Smoke-tested end-to-end via CLI: keyword forms work, bot-only fatal at init, path forms rejected at init with the right hint, bare-name typo gives the disambiguation message at parse time.

Pre-commit clean: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 270 tests pass (257 baseline + 13 new), `cargo install --locked`, `vc-x1 --version` → `0.42.0-2`.

Please review.

> AGENT

Proposed commit message for `vc-x1 push main`:

**Title:**
```
feat: --scope parser + init retrofit (0.42.0-2)
```

**Body:**
```
Lands the shared `--scope` value parser and migrates `init` onto
the new `Scope` enum. Parser is the boundary every remaining
`--scope` consumer (sync/push/finalize/clone in -3+) will route
through; init exercises the keyword path end-to-end. Path form
is parser-accepted but rejected at init with a hint pointing at
`--repo-local` / `--repo-remote`.

- Cargo.toml: 0.42.0-1 → 0.42.0-2.
- src/scope.rs: `pub fn parse_scope(s: &str) -> Result<Scope, String>`.
  Accepts the four keyword forms + path forms prefixed by
  `./`, `../`, `/`, `~/`, or bare `~`; bare unprefixed names
  error with a `./X` disambiguation hint. Dropped the
  now-dead `is_empty` helper. 13 new parser tests. Removed
  `#[allow(dead_code)]` on `Single` (parser constructs it).
- src/init.rs: `--scope` field `Option<Vec<Side>>` →
  `Option<Scope>` with `value_parser = crate::scope::parse_scope`.
  `plan_init` matches on the enum: `Roles(_)` keeps the
  bot-only-fatal check; `Single(p)` errors with the
  init-specific hint. `fixture_scoped` signature + 5 call
  sites + `scope_parses_*` tests migrated. New
  `scope_path_form_rejected_at_init` test.
- notes/chores-07.md: `### 0.42.0-2:` subsection.
- notes/todo.md: In Progress — `-1 (done)`, `-2 (current)`.
```

1	use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
2	use std::process::Command;
3	
4	use clap::Args;
5	use log::{LevelFilter, debug, info, warn};
6	
7	use crate::common::{default_scope, find_workspace_root, run, scope_to_repos};
8	use crate::scope::{Scope, Side};
9	
10	/// Fetch and sync a set of repos to their remotes.
11	///
12	/// Default is `--check`: fetch + classify + report, **error** if any
13	/// repo is `behind` or `diverged`. Pass `--no-check` to actually
14	/// rebase / fast-forward. On any failure the starting state of every
15	/// repo is restored via `jj op restore`.
16	///
17	/// Scripts and automation should pass `--check` or `--no-check`
18	/// explicitly rather than relying on the default — defaults can shift,
19	/// explicit flags lock in the contract.
20	///
21	/// Repo set is resolved (in order):
22	///
23	/// - `-R` / `--repo` → exactly that list (back-compat / arbitrary multi-repo).
24	/// - `--scope=code|bot|code,bot` → dual-repo roles, resolved via the
25	///   project root's `.vc-config.toml`.
26	/// - Neither → default scope: `code,bot` when `.vc-config.toml` has a
27	///   non-empty `[workspace] other-repo`, else `code`. POR (no
28	///   `.vc-config.toml`) → `code` resolved to cwd.
29	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
30	pub struct SyncArgs {
31	    /// Verify only — fetch + classify; error if any repo needs action.
32	    /// This is the default; pass explicitly in scripts.
33	    #[arg(long, conflicts_with = "no_check")]
34	    pub check: bool,
35	
36	    /// Apply — fetch + classify, then rebase / fast-forward as needed.
37	    #[arg(long, conflicts_with = "check")]
38	    pub no_check: bool,
39	
40	    /// Suppress all informational output (exit code signals result)
41	    #[arg(short, long)]
42	    pub quiet: bool,
43	
44	    /// Bookmark to sync in each repo
45	    #[arg(long, default_value = "main")]
46	    pub bookmark: String,
47	
48	    /// Remote to sync against
49	    #[arg(long, default_value = "origin")]
50	    pub remote: String,
51	
52	    /// Path to jj repo; repeatable or comma-separated.
53	    ///
54	    /// Mutually exclusive with `--scope`. When neither is given the
55	    /// repo set is derived from `--scope` defaults.
56	    #[arg(
57	        short = 'R',
58	        long = "repo",
59	        value_name = "PATH",
60	        conflicts_with = "scope"
61	    )]
62	    pub repos: Vec<PathBuf>,
63	
64	    /// Which repo(s) of the dual-repo to sync.
65	    ///
66	    /// `SCOPE=code|bot|code,bot`:
67	    ///
68	    /// - `code` — sync only the app repo.
69	    /// - `bot` — sync only the bot repo (errors if no bot repo
70	    ///   is configured).
71	    /// - `code,bot` — sync both repos (the default when both are
72	    ///   configured).
73	    #[arg(
74	        long,
75	        value_name = "SCOPE",
76	        value_delimiter = ',',
77	        verbatim_doc_comment
78	    )]
79	    pub scope: Option<Vec<Side>>,
80	}
81	
82	/// Split the caller's `-R` flags into a concrete repo list.
83	///
84	/// Each value is split on `,` and trimmed, so `-R .,.claude` works
85	/// identically to `-R . -R .claude`. Caller is responsible for not
86	/// passing an empty slice — `sync()` consults `--scope` instead.
87	fn split_repos(raw: &[PathBuf]) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
88	    raw.iter()
89	        .flat_map(|p| {
90	            p.to_string_lossy()
91	                .split(',')
92	                .map(|s| PathBuf::from(s.trim()))
93	                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
94	        })
95	        .collect()
96	}
97	
98	/// Resolve `args` into the concrete repo list `sync_repos` operates on.
99	///
100	/// Precedence: explicit `-R` → `--scope` → workspace-default scope.
101	/// See [`SyncArgs`] for the full contract.
102	fn resolve_args_to_repos(args: &SyncArgs) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
103	    if !args.repos.is_empty() {
104	        return Ok(split_repos(&args.repos));
105	    }
106	    let workspace_root = find_workspace_root();
107	    let scope = match &args.scope {
108	        Some(sides) if sides.is_empty() => return Err("--scope: value is empty".into()),
109	        Some(sides) => Scope::Roles(sides.clone()),
110	        None => default_scope(workspace_root.as_deref()),
111	    };
112	    scope_to_repos(&scope, workspace_root.as_deref())
113	}
114	
115	/// Relationship between a local bookmark and its remote counterpart.

845	    //! End-to-end tests for `sync_repos` against real dual-repo jj
846	    //! fixtures. Each test builds an isolated fixture (bare-git

850	    //! `jj` subprocess calls. Requires `jj` in `PATH`.
851	    //!
852	    //! Fixtures clean themselves up via `Fixture`'s `Drop` impl so a
853	    //! panicking test still removes its tempdir.

862	    /// Resolver helpers must consume what `init --repo-local` produces.
863	    ///
864	    /// Builds a real dual-repo fixture (bare git remotes + colocated jj
865	    /// repos + the canonical `.vc-config.toml` pair init writes), then
866	    /// drives the resolver chain `sync()` uses when `-R` is empty:
867	    ///
868	    /// - `find_workspace_root_from(&fx.claude)` walks up to `fx.work`
869	    ///   (proves cwd-portability against init's `path = "/.claude"` /
870	    ///   `path = "/"` config split).
871	    /// - `default_scope(Some(&fx.work))` reads the workspace config
872	    ///   and resolves to the dual-repo default.
873	    /// - `scope_to_repos` maps each `Scope` shape to the right
874	    ///   absolute path(s) under the fixture.
875	    ///
876	    /// Pure check on the resolver chain — does not invoke `sync()`
877	    /// itself, since that walks `std::env::current_dir()` and parallel
878	    /// `cargo test` makes cwd mutation unsafe.
879	    #[test]
880	    fn resolver_chain_against_init_repo_local() {
881	        let fx = Fixture::new("resolver-chain");
882	
883	        // Walk-up from the bot side lands on the app root.
884	        assert_eq!(
885	            find_workspace_root_from(&fx.claude).as_deref(),
886	            Some(&*fx.work),
887	            "find_workspace_root should resolve from .claude up to work"
888	        );
889	        // Walk-up from the app root finds itself.

When neither is given the\n    /// repo set is derived from `--scope` defaults.\n    #[arg(\n        short = 'R',\n        long = \"repo\",\n        value_name = \"PATH\",\n        conflicts_with = \"scope\"\n    )]\n    pub repos: Vec<PathBuf>,\n\n    /// Which repo(s) of the dual-repo to sync.\n    ///\n    /// `SCOPE=code|bot|code,bot`:\n    ///\n    /// - `code` — sync only the app repo.\n    /// - `bot` — sync only the bot repo (errors if no bot repo\n    ///   is configured).\n    /// - `code,bot` — sync both repos (the default when both are\n    ///   configured).\n    #[arg(\n        long,\n        value_name = \"SCOPE\",\n        value_delimiter = ',',\n        verbatim_doc_comment\n    )]\n    pub scope: Option<Vec<Side>>,\n}\n\n/// Split the caller's `-R` flags into a concrete repo list.\n///\n/// Each value is split on `,` and trimmed, so `-R .,.claude` works\n/// identically to `-R . -R .claude`. Caller is responsible for not\n/// passing an empty slice — `sync()` consults `--scope` instead.\nfn split_repos(raw: &[PathBuf]) -> Vec<PathBuf> {\n    raw.iter()\n        .flat_map(|p| {\n            p.to_string_lossy()\n                .split(',')\n                .map(|s| PathBuf::from(s.trim()))\n                .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n        })\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// Resolve `args` into the concrete repo list `sync_repos` operates on.\n///\n/// Precedence: explicit `-R` → `--scope` → workspace-default scope.\n/// See [`SyncArgs`] for the full contract.\nfn resolve_args_to_repos(args: &SyncArgs) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    if !args.repos.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(split_repos(&args.repos));\n    }\n    let workspace_root = find_workspace_root();\n    let scope = match &args.scope {\n        Some(sides) if sides.is_empty() => return Err(\"--scope: value is empty\".into()),\n        Some(sides) => Scope::Roles(sides.clone()),\n        None => default_scope(workspace_root.as_deref()),\n    };\n    scope_to_repos(&scope, workspace_root.as_deref())\n}",
  "new_string": "use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::process::Command;\n\nuse clap::Args;\nuse log::{LevelFilter, debug, info, warn};\n\nuse crate::common::{default_scope, find_workspace_root, run, scope_to_repos};\nuse crate::scope::{Scope, parse_scope};\n\n/// Fetch and sync a set of repos to their remotes.\n///\n/// Default is `--check`: fetch + classify + report, **error** if any\n/// repo is `behind` or `diverged`. Pass `--no-check` to actually\n/// rebase / fast-forward. On any failure the starting state of every\n/// repo is restored via `jj op restore`.\n///\n/// Scripts and automation should pass `--check` or `--no-check`\n/// explicitly rather than relying on the default — defaults can shift,\n/// explicit flags lock in the contract.\n///\n/// Repo set is resolved from `--scope`:\n///\n/// - Explicit `--scope=code|bot|code,bot|<path>` — keyword forms\n///   resolve via the project root's `.vc-config.toml`; the path\n///   form syncs that one repo (single-repo mode).\n/// - No `--scope` — workspace-default scope:\n///   - dual workspace (`.vc-config.toml` with `other-repo`) → `code,bot`\n///   - single-repo workspace (`.vc-config.toml`, no `other-repo`) → `code`\n///   - POR (no `.vc-config.toml`) → cwd as a single-repo target\n#[derive(Args, Debug)]\npub struct SyncArgs {\n    /// Verify only — fetch + classify; error if any repo needs action.\n    /// This is the default; pass explicitly in scripts.\n    #[arg(long, conflicts_with = \"no_check\")]\n    pub check: bool,\n\n    /// Apply — fetch + classify, then rebase / fast-forward as needed.\n    #[arg(long, conflicts_with = \"check\")]\n    pub no_check: bool,\n\n    /// Suppress all informational output (exit code signals result)\n    #[arg(short, long)]\n    pub quiet: bool,\n\n    /// Bookmark to sync in each repo\n    #[arg(long, default_value = \"main\")]\n    pub bookmark: String,\n\n    /// Remote to sync against\n    #[arg(long, default_value = \"origin\")]\n    pub remote: String,\n\n    /// Which repo(s) of the workspace to sync.\n    ///\n    /// `SCOPE=code|bot|code,bot|<path>`:\n    ///\n    /// - `code` — sync only the app repo.\n    /// - `bot` — sync only the bot repo (errors if no bot repo\n    ///   is configured).\n    /// - `code,bot` — sync both repos.\n    /// - `<path>` (`./X`, `/X`, `~/X`) — single-repo mode; sync\n    ///   only that repo, ignoring `.vc-config.toml`.\n    ///\n    /// Default depends on workspace state: dual workspace →\n    /// `code,bot`; single-repo workspace → `code`; POR → cwd.\n    #[arg(\n        short = 's',\n        long,\n        value_name = \"SCOPE\",\n        value_parser = parse_scope,\n        verbatim_doc_comment\n    )]\n    pub scope: Option<Scope>,\n}\n\n/// Resolve `args` into the concrete repo list `sync_repos` operates on.\n///\n/// Explicit `--scope` wins; otherwise resolves via `default_scope`\n/// against the discovered workspace root and cwd.\nfn resolve_args_to_repos(args: &SyncArgs) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let workspace_root = find_workspace_root();\n    let cwd = std::env::current_dir()?;\n    let scope = match &args.scope {\n        Some(s) => s.clone(),\n        None => default_scope(workspace_root.as_deref(), &cwd),\n    };\n    scope_to_repos(&scope, workspace_root.as_deref())\n}"
}

  "old_string": "/// Resolve the workspace's default `--scope`.\n///\n/// Reads `<workspace_root>/.vc-config.toml > [workspace] other-repo`:\n///\n/// - non-empty `other-repo` → `Roles([Code, Bot])`\n/// - missing / empty `other-repo` → `Roles([Code])`\n/// - `workspace_root = None` (POR) → `Roles([Code])`\n///\n/// Single-repo / POR refinement to `Single(_)` lands in 0.42.0-3.\npub fn default_scope(workspace_root: Option<&Path>) -> Scope {\n    let Some(root) = workspace_root else {\n        return Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code]);\n    };\n    let cfg = match toml_simple::toml_load(&root.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE)) {\n        Ok(c) => c,\n        Err(_) => return Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code]),\n    };\n    match toml_simple::toml_get(&cfg, \"workspace.other-repo\") {\n        Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code, Side::Bot]),\n        _ => Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code]),\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Resolve the workspace's default `--scope`.\n///\n/// Reads `<workspace_root>/.vc-config.toml > [workspace] other-repo`:\n///\n/// - dual workspace (non-empty `other-repo`) → `Roles([Code, Bot])`\n/// - single-repo workspace (missing / empty `other-repo`) → `Roles([Code])`\n/// - POR (no workspace_root) → `Single(cwd)` — operate on cwd as a\n///   one-off jj repo, ignoring any workspace context.\npub fn default_scope(workspace_root: Option<&Path>, cwd: &Path) -> Scope {\n    let Some(root) = workspace_root else {\n        return Scope::Single(cwd.to_path_buf());\n    };\n    let cfg = match toml_simple::toml_load(&root.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE)) {\n        Ok(c) => c,\n        Err(_) => return Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code]),\n    };\n    match toml_simple::toml_get(&cfg, \"workspace.other-repo\") {\n        Some(v) if !v.is_empty() => Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code, Side::Bot]),\n        _ => Scope::Roles(vec![Side::Code]),\n    }\n}"
}

875	    /// Add a local-only commit on `main` in `repo` (not pushed), then
876	    /// restore `@` to an empty child so pre-flight still passes.
877	    ///
878	    /// Sequence: write file → describe `@` → advance `main` to `@` →
879	    /// create a fresh empty `@` above it.
880	    fn add_local_commit(repo: &Path, file: &str, content: &str, msg: &str) {
881	        fs::write(repo.join(file), content).expect("write local file");
882	        jj(repo, &["describe", "@", "-m", msg]);
883	        jj(repo, &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@"]);
884	        jj(repo, &["new"]);
885	    }
886	
887	    /// Clone `remote_url` into `<base>/<work_name>`, add a commit, push it.
888	    ///
889	    /// Used to make the remote advance beyond the fixture's `main`
890	    /// from a separate working copy. Returns the pushed commit's id.
891	    fn push_from_clone(
892	        base: &Path,
893	        remote_url: &Path,
894	        work_name: &str,
895	        file: &str,
896	        content: &str,
897	        msg: &str,
898	    ) -> String {
899	        let workdir = base.join(work_name);
900	        let out = Command::new("jj")
901	            .args(["git", "clone", "--colocate"])
902	            .arg(remote_url)
903	            .arg(&workdir)
904	            .output()
905	            .expect("spawn jj clone");
906	        assert!(
907	            out.status.success(),
908	            "jj git clone failed: {}",
909	            String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)
910	        );
911	        fs::write(workdir.join(file), content).expect("write remote file");
912	        jj(&workdir, &["describe", "@", "-m", msg]);
913	        jj(&workdir, &["bookmark", "set", "main", "-r", "@"]);
914	        jj(&workdir, &["git", "push", "--bookmark", "main"]);
915	        cid(&workdir, "main")
916	    }

> AGENT

Work complete for 0.42.0-3.

**Cargo.toml** — `0.42.0-2` → `0.42.0-3`.

**src/common.rs**:
- `default_scope` signature gains `cwd: &Path`. POR branch (no workspace_root) returns `Scope::Single(cwd.to_path_buf())` instead of `Roles([Code])`. Workspace branches unchanged.
- `scope_to_repos`'s `Single(p)` arm resolves to `vec![p.clone()]` — no workspace lookup; the path is whatever the caller stored.
- Tests: `default_scope_por` → `default_scope_por_returns_single_cwd`; staging-error test → `scope_to_repos_single_returns_path` (happy path); 3 other `default_scope_*` tests pass `Path::new(".")` for the irrelevant cwd parameter.

**src/sync.rs**:
- `-R/--repo` flag and `repos: Vec<PathBuf>` field deleted. `split_repos` helper and its 4 unit tests go away.
- `--scope` retypes `Option<Vec<Side>>` → `Option<Scope>` with `value_parser = parse_scope`, gains `short = 's'`.
- SyncArgs doc rewritten around the new resolution rules (no more `-R`, three-state default).
- `resolve_args_to_repos` simplified — drops `args.repos` branch, feeds cwd into `default_scope`.
- Tests: `parse_scope_*` migrated to `Some(Scope::Roles(...))` shape; new `parse_scope_path_form` and `parse_scope_short_form`; obsolete repo-flag tests removed. `apply_args` integration helper drops the `repos` field. `Side` import gated `#[cfg(test)]`.

**notes/chores-07.md** — added `### 0.42.0-3: sync --scope retrofit` subsection.

**notes/todo.md** — In Progress: `-2 (done)`, `-3 (current)`.

Smoke-tested end-to-end:
- `vc-x1 sync --check` (dual ws) → 2 repos up-to-date.
- `-s code` / `-s bot` → 1 repo each.
- `-s ./.claude` → 1 repo via Single-mode (bypasses workspace lookup).
- `cd /tmp && vc-x1 sync --check` → POR detected, default_scope returned `Single(/tmp)` (errors on the non-jj path, as expected).

Pre-commit clean: fmt, clippy `-D warnings`, 265 tests pass (270 in -2; net -5 from removed `-R` tests minus 2 added). `vc-x1 sync -h` shows `-s, --scope` and no `-R`.

Please review.

> DEVELOPER

go

> AGENT

Proposed commit message for `vc-x1 push main`:

**Title:**
```
refactor: sync --scope retrofit, drop -R (0.42.0-3)
```

**Body:**
```
Migrates `sync` onto `Scope` and lands the Single(_) resolver
wiring. `-R/--repo` retires (single-repo callers move to
`--scope=<path>`); `-s` becomes the short form. Default-scope
rules pick up the three-state model: dual workspace →
Roles([Code, Bot]), single-repo workspace → Roles([Code]),
POR → Single(cwd).

- Cargo.toml: 0.42.0-2 → 0.42.0-3.
- src/common.rs: `default_scope(workspace_root, cwd)` — POR
  branch returns `Single(cwd)`. `scope_to_repos`'s `Single(p)`
  arm resolves to `vec![p]` directly. Tests: POR test
  renamed; staging-error test → happy-path
  `scope_to_repos_single_returns_path`; 3 other tests pass
  `Path::new(".")` for the irrelevant cwd.
- src/sync.rs: `-R/--repo` field + `split_repos` helper +
  4 unit tests deleted. `--scope` retypes
  `Option<Vec<Side>>` → `Option<Scope>` with
  `value_parser = parse_scope`, gains `short = 's'`. SyncArgs
  doc rewritten. `resolve_args_to_repos` simplified, feeds
  cwd into `default_scope`. New `parse_scope_path_form` and
  `parse_scope_short_form` tests; obsolete repo-flag tests
  removed. `apply_args` drops `repos`. `Side` import gated
  `#[cfg(test)]`.
- notes/chores-07.md: `### 0.42.0-3:` subsection.
- notes/todo.md: In Progress — `-2 (done)`, `-3 (current)`.
```

Approve to run `vc-x1 push main`?

> DEVELOPER

For shortterm backwards compatibility can we keep -R,--repo is that an issue?

> AGENT

Not technically hard — add `-R/--repo` back as a `Vec<PathBuf>` flag with `conflicts_with = "scope"` and a `-R` branch in `resolve_args_to_repos` (essentially revert the deletions). The trade-off: it backs out the 0.41.0-3 commitment ("intentional break, not a deprecation") and gives two ways to spell single-repo (`-R /path` vs `--scope=/path`), which is exactly what the consistency-everywhere principle was trimming out. My recommendation is to either (a) hold the line — pre-1.0, no external users yet, soft transition is cheap to skip — or (b) keep `-R` as a stickier deprecation: prints a one-time `warn!` pointing at `--scope=<path>`, routes through the new resolver, scheduled for removal in 0.43.0. Which do you want?

> DEVELOPER

Ok let's keep the current plan, we'll just get a hard error if actor-x1 tries to use it, correct?

> AGENT

Correct — clap rejects unknown args at parse time. If actor-x1 invokes `vc-x1 sync -R /path/to/repo`, it'll get:

```
error: unexpected argument '-R' found

Usage: vc-x1 sync [OPTIONS]

For more information, try '--help'.
```

Non-zero exit code, message on stderr. So the failure is loud (subprocess invocation fails) rather than silent (sync silently does the wrong thing). If actor-x1 owns its own copy of the CLI surface, you'll just update its `-R` call sites to `--scope=<path>` (or `-s <path>`) at the same time you ship 0.42.0-3.

Want me to proceed with the original commit message?

> DEVELOPER

yes
